## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我去看一个项目里挂掉的测试，弄清原因后告诉你。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your partner on this Mac. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos and tell you when it's clear.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 지금 바로 해드릴 수 있는 일은 예를 들어 특정 저장소의 깨진 테스트를 고치거나, 웹에서 정보를 찾아보거나, 메일과 캘린더를 확인하는 것처럼 실제 작업을 맡기는 거예요. 어떤 일부터 해볼까요?
```

```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼性を重視して確実に届ける」、UDPは「速さを優先して届けっぱなし」が一番の違いです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar o actualizar tus propias ramas que aún no has compartido, dejando el historial lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o quieres conservar tal cual cuándo se integró el trabajo, y nunca reescribas commits que otros ya tengan.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、保证可靠的：数据有序、不丢不重，代价是要先握手、有重传和拥塞控制。UDP 是无连接、不保证可靠的：尽快发出去，不管丢没丢，延迟低、开销小。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase to tidy your own unpublished branch before it lands, so history reads as one clean line. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, because rewriting public history breaks everyone else's copy.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用 `bun test` 后面加上文件路径就行，例如：

```sh
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

路径可以写全，也可以只写文件名的一部分，bun 会匹配包含这段字符串的测试文件。想只跑某个测试用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：

```sh
bun test -t "某个用例名"
```

两者可以组合，比如 `bun test test/foo.test.ts -t "某个用例名"`。
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database first records every change it intends to make, before touching the main data. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart to recover committed work and roll back anything half-finished.
```

    <div class="k-sum-label">周五北京到上海 · 600 元以下</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="best"><span class="k-none">还没看</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新一次</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">每天的班次</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<style>
  .k-row-value.k-dim { color: var(--d-muted); }
  .k-row-sub.k-warn { color: var(--d-bad); }
</style>
<script>
  function fmt(n) { return '¥' + n; }
  function render(days) {
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var best = document.getElementById('best');
    var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    if (!days || !days.length) {
      list.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">还没有记录</div>';
      return;
    }
    var latest = days[days.length - 1];
    var cheap = (latest.trains || []).filter(function (t) { return t.price < 600; })
      .sort(function (a, b) { return a.price - b.price; });
    if (cheap.length) {
      best.textContent = fmt(cheap[0].price) + ' · ' + cheap[0].no;
      sub.textContent = latest.label + '，600 元以下有 ' + cheap.length + ' 班';
    } else {
      best.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">暂无 600 以下</span>';
      sub.textContent = latest.label + '，还没有低于 600 的班次';
    }
    days.slice().reverse().forEach(function (d) {
      (d.trains || []).forEach(function (t) {
        var row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = 'k-row';
        row.innerHTML =
          '<div class="k-row-main">' + t.no + ' · ' + d.label + '</div>' +
          '<div class="k-row-sub' + (t.seats && t.left && t.left <= 5 ? ' k-warn' : '') + '">' +
            (t.seats || '') + (t.left ? '，余 ' + t.left + ' 张' : '') + '</div>' +
          '<div class="k-row-value' + (t.price >= 600 ? ' k-dim' : '') + '">' + fmt(t.price) + '</div>';
        list.appendChild(row);
      });
    });
  }
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('days')).then(render);
  ducter.onChange(function () { Promise.resolve(ducter.get('days')).then(render); });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"days":[{"label":"第 3 天","trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":"二等座","left":12},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":"二等座","left":4},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":"二等座","left":null}]}]}

    val.className = 'k-row-value';

    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>

    $('#w').value = x.what;
    $('#a').value = x.amount;
    $('#addtitle').textContent = '改这一笔';

    $('#cancel').style.display = '';
    $('#addbox').open = true;
    $('#addbox').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });

    var stored = ducter.get('expenses');
    items = Array.isArray(stored) ? stored : [];
    render();
  }

  ducter.onChange(load);
  resetForm();
  load();
})();

<h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty">还没记</div>
</div>
<details class="k-add" id="addbox">
  <summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-field"><label for="what">花了什么</label><input class="k-input" id="what" placeholder="比如 午饭、打车"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="amt">金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="date">日期</label><input class="k-input" id="date" placeholder="11/20"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记上</button>
    <button class="k-btn" id="del" hidden>删除这笔</button>
  </div>
</details>
</main>
<script>
let items = [], editing = -1;
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const fmt = n => '¥' + Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('zh-CN');
const esc = s => String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));

function load() {
  con … ary(items.length ? items.length + ' 笔,合计 ' + fmt(total) : '还没记');
}

function reset() {
  editing = -1;
  $('what').value = '';
  $('amt').value = '';
  $('date').value = '';
  $('save').textContent = '记上';
  $('del').hidden = true;
  $('addbox').open = false;
}

function persist() {
  ducter.set('expenses', items);
  render();
}

$('list').onclick = e => {
  const row = e.target.closest('[data-i]');
  if (!row) return;
  editing = Number(row.dataset.i);
  const x = items[editing];
  $('what').value = x.what || '';
  $('amt').value = x.amount;
  $('date').value = x.date || '';
  $('save').textContent = '改好了';
  $('del').hidden = false;
  $('addbox').open = true;
};

$('save').onclick = () => {
  const what = $('what').value.trim();
  const amount = parseFloat($('amt').value);
  if (!what || !(amount >= 0)) return;
  const rec = { what: what, amount: amount, date: $('date').value.trim() };
  if (editing >= 0) {
    rec.from = items[editing].from;
    items[editing] = rec;
  } else {
    items.push(rec);
  }
  reset();
  persist();
};

$('del').onclick = () => {
  if (editing < 0) return;
  items.splice(editing, 1);
  reset();
  persist();
};

ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁 G1 上海虹桥→北京南","amount":662,"date":"11/20","from":"你的预订"},{"what":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","amount":1036,"date":"11/20","from":"你的预订"}]}

<script>
(function(){
  var boxes = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('input[data-id]'));
  var total = boxes.length;
  function landedList(){ return boxes.filter(function(b){return b.checked;}).map(function(b){return b.dataset.id;}); }
  function render(landed){
    if(!Array.isArray(landed)) landed = [];
    boxes.forEach(function(b){
      b.checked = landed.indexOf(b.dataset.id) !== -1;
      b.closest('.k-row').classList.toggle('done', b.checked);
    });
    var left = total - landedList().length;
    document.getElementById('left').textContent = left;
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = (total - left) + ' landed so far';
    if (typeof ducter !== 'undefined' && ducter.summary) ducter.summary(left + ' waiting');
  }
  function load(){ return Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(render); }
  boxes.forEach(function(b){
    b.addEventListener('change', function(){
      var landed = landedList();
      ducter.set('landed', landed);
      render(landed);
    });
  });
  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","text":"wey-gu/ducter#412 · compaction cut","status":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday","from":"Your PRs, 2026-11-24"},{"id":"mem-10287","text":"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · retry on 502","status":"On CI, failing","from":"Your PRs, 2026-11-24"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","text":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · flaky compaction test","status":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","from":"Your PRs, 2026-11-24"},{"id":"ducter-418","text":"wey-gu/ducter#418 · apps bar","status":"Draft","from":"Your PRs, 2026-11-24"}],"landed":[]}
